# Roth/GenWronskian.lean / Roth/Taylor.lean

418 comparisons among the 293 accepted declarations. [Source hashes and review provenance](GenWronskian--Taylor.json).

| `MvPolynomial.taylor` | `Roth.genWronskian` | no | A: Defines multivariate polynomial translation by a vector as a bundled coefficient-algebra endomorphism. Mechanism: The universal evaluation homomorphism aeval substitutes X j+C(α j) for each variable, automatically preserving sums, products, and coefficients. B: Defines the determinant whose rows apply prescribed multivariate Hasse derivatives to a finite polynomial family. Mechanism: Forms a square Matrix.of of hasseDeriv entries and takes its determinant. Taylor and Kronecker are both universal substitutions, but Taylor shifts generators independently and has a ring inverse; Kronecker collapses many variables to powers of one and is only injective on bounded-degree subspaces. Its Hasse-span argument studies derivatives of the substituted polynomial and needs chain-rule coefficients, unlike translation's simple additive composition. | Share aeval/monomial/substitution laws; preserve bounded injectivity and chain-rule operator spans separately from global translation inversion. |
| `MvPolynomial.taylor` | `Roth.kronecker` | no | A: Defines multivariate polynomial translation by a vector as a bundled coefficient-algebra endomorphism. Mechanism: The universal evaluation homomorphism aeval substitutes X j+C(α j) for each variable, automatically preserving sums, products, and coefficients. B: Defines substitution of successive base-k powers of one indeterminate for an enumerated finite set of variables. Mechanism: Uses MvPolynomial.aeval at X^(k^e(j)), where Fintype.equivFin supplies the enumeration. Taylor and Kronecker are both universal substitutions, but Taylor shifts generators independently and has a ring inverse; Kronecker collapses many variables to powers of one and is only injective on bounded-degree subspaces.
